Mathlib.Data.Nat.Prime

Statements of Mathlib (commit 6972d52c0d), each with its Lean source, its translation to Dedukti by Export.lean, and its informalization by Informath, in English, as a formula, and in French, Czech, German and Swedish, which the buttons at each entry show there, and the check boxes above the entries everywhere. The Dedukti translation drops implicit and instance arguments and maps Lean's arithmetic and logic to Informath's base (mapping.tsv); the other Mathlib constants are worded by the symbol table mathlib.dkgf, whose English wordings Informath also renders in the other languages. Constants without a wording appear as $\mathrm{name}$.

Nat.PrimedefDefs.lean:42
Nat.Prime p means that p is a prime number, that is, a natural number at least 2 whose only divisors are p and 1. The theorem Nat.prime_def witnesses this description of a prime number.
Lean
@[pp_nodot, wikidata Q49008, oeis A000040]
def Prime (p : ℕ) :=
  Irreducible p
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__Prime : (p : Elem Nat) -> Prop :=
  p => Irreducible Nat p.
English
Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if $p$ is irreducible.
French
Soit $p \in \Natural$. Alors $p$ est premier si $p$ est irréductible.
Czech
Nechť $p \in \Natural$. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, jestliže $p$ je ireducibilní.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \operatorname{Prime} (p):\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{Irreducible} (p)$

Nat.Prime.eq_one_or_self_of_dvdtheoremDefs.lean:88
Lean
theorem Prime.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd {p : ℕ} (pp : p.Prime) (m : ℕ) (hm : m ∣ p) :
    m = 1 ∨ m = p := by
  obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
  have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
  rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
  apply Or.imp_right _ this
  rintro rfl
  rw [hn, mul_one]
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__Prime__eq_one_or_self_of_dvd : (p : Elem Nat) -> (pp : Proof (prime p)) -> (m : Elem Nat) -> (hm : Proof (divide m p)) -> Proof (or (Eq m 1) (Eq m p)).
English
Let $p , m \in \Natural$ such that $p$ is prime and $m$ divides $p$. Then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.
French
Soit $p , m \in \Natural$ tel que $p$ est premier et $m$ divise $p$. Alors $m = 1$ ou $m = p$.
Czech
Nechť $p , m \in \Natural$ takový, že $p$ je prvočíselné a $m$ dělí $p$. Tedy $m = 1$ nebo $m = p$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural , \mathrm{prime} (p), m \in \Natural , \divides {m}{p}\vdash m = 1 \vee m = p$
Proof
6 lines
1.Let $n \in \Natural$ such that $p = m \times n$.obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
2.$m$ is a unit or $n$ is a unit.have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
3.By rewriting, $m = 1$ or $n = 1$.rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
4.It is enough to show that if $n = 1$, then $m = p$.apply Or.imp_right _ this
5.To prove that $m = p$, assume that $n = 1$.rintro rfl
6.By rewriting, $m = p$.rw [hn, mul_one]
Proof, French
6 lines
1.Soit $n \in \Natural$ tel que $p = m \times n$.obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
2.$m$ est une unité ou $n$ est une unité.have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
3.Par réécriture, $m = 1$ ou $n = 1$.rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
4.Il suffit de montrer que si $n = 1$, alors $m = p$.apply Or.imp_right _ this
5.Pour démontrer que $m = p$, supposons que $n = 1$.rintro rfl
6.Par réécriture, $m = p$.rw [hn, mul_one]
Proof, Czech
6 lines
1.Nechť $n \in \Natural$ takový, že $p = m \times n$.obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := hm
2.$m$ je jednotka nebo $n$ je jednotka.have := pp.isUnit_or_isUnit hn
3.Přepsáním, $m = 1$ nebo $n = 1$.rw [Nat.isUnit_iff, Nat.isUnit_iff] at this
4.Stačí ukázat, že jestliže $n = 1$, pak $m = p$.apply Or.imp_right _ this
5.Abychom dokázali, že $m = p$, předpokládejme, že $n = 1$.rintro rfl
6.Přepsáním, $m = p$.rw [hn, mul_one]
Nat.prime_deftheoremDefs.lean:98
Nat.Prime p means that p is a prime number, that is, a natural number at least 2 whose only divisors are p and 1. The theorem Nat.prime_def witnesses this description of a prime number.
Lean
@[inherit_doc Nat.Prime]
theorem prime_def {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, m ∣ p → m = 1 ∨ m = p := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
  have h1 := Nat.one_lt_two.trans_le h.1
  refine ⟨mt Nat.isUnit_iff.mp h1.ne', ?_⟩
  rintro a b rfl
  simp only [Nat.isUnit_iff]
  refine (h.2 a <| dvd_mul_right ..).imp_right fun hab ↦ ?_
  rw [← mul_right_inj' (Nat.ne_zero_of_lt h1), ← hab, ← hab, mul_one]
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__prime_def : (p : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (prime p) (and (Leq 2 p) (forall Nat (m => if (divide m p) (or (Eq m 1) (Eq m p)))))).
English
Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m$ divides $p$, then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.
French
Soit $p \in \Natural$. Alors $p$ est premier si et seulement si $2 \leq p$ et pour tous les nombres naturels $m$, si $m$ divise $p$, alors $m = 1$ ou $m = p$.
Czech
Nechť $p \in \Natural$. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, právě tehdy, když $2 \leq p$ a pro všechna přirozená čísla $m$, jestliže $m$ dělí $p$, pak $m = 1$ nebo $m = p$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \mathrm{prime} (p)\leftrightarrow 2 \leq p \wedge (\forall m \in \Natural . \divides {m}{p}\rightarrow m = 1 \vee m = p)$
Proof
9 lines
1.Assume that $2 \leq p$ and for all elements $m$ of $\Natural$, if $\divides {m}{p}$, then $m = 1$ or $m = p$.refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
2.It is enough to show that $p$ is prime.refine ⟨fun h => ⟨h.two_le, h.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
3.$1 < p$.have h1 := Nat.one_lt_two.trans_le h.1
4.It is enough to show that for all elements $a$ and $b$ of $\Natural$, if $p = a \times b$, then $a$ is a unit or $b$ is a unit.refine ⟨mt Nat.isUnit_iff.mp h1.ne', ?_⟩
5.Let $a , b \in \Natural$.rintro a b rfl
6.It is enough to show that $a = 1$ or $b = 1$.simp only [Nat.isUnit_iff]
7.Assume that $a = a \times b$.refine (h.2 a <| dvd_mul_right ..).imp_right fun hab ↦ ?_
8.It is enough to show that $b = 1$.refine (h.2 a <| dvd_mul_right ..).imp_right fun hab ↦ ?_
9.By rewriting, $b = 1$.rw [← mul_right_inj' (Nat.ne_zero_of_lt h1), ← hab, ← hab, mul_one]

Nat.prime_def_lttheoremDefs.lean:107
Lean
theorem prime_def_lt {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m < p, m ∣ p → m = 1 :=
  prime_def.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      forall_congr' fun _ =>
        ⟨fun h l d => (h d).resolve_right (ne_of_lt l), fun h d =>
          (le_of_dvd (le_of_succ_le p2) d).lt_or_eq_dec.imp_left fun l => h l d⟩
Dedukti
2 lines
def Nat__prime_def_lt : (p : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (prime p) (and (Leq 2 p) (forall Nat (m => if (Lt m p) (if (divide m p) (Eq m 1)))))) :=
  p => Iff__trans (prime p) (and (Leq 2 p) (forall Nat (m => if (divide m p) (or (Eq m 1) (Eq m p))))) (and (Leq 2 p) (forall Nat (m1 => if (Lt m1 p) (if (divide m1 p) (Eq m1 1))))) (Nat__prime_def p) (and_congr_right (Leq 2 p) (forall Nat (m2 => if (divide m2 p) (or (Eq m2 1) (Eq m2 p)))) (forall Nat (m3 => if (Lt m3 p) (if (divide m3 p) (Eq m3 1)))) (ifI (Leq 2 p) (iff (forall Nat (a => if (divide a p) (or (Eq a 1) (Eq a p)))) (forall Nat (a1 => if (Lt a1 p) (if (divide a1 p) (Eq a1 1))))) (p2 => forall_congr' Nat (m4 => if (divide m4 p) (or (Eq m4 1) (Eq m4 p))) (m5 => if (Lt m5 p) (if (divide m5 p) (Eq m5 1))) (forallI Nat (x => iff (if (divide x p) (or (Eq x 1) (Eq x p))) (if (Lt x p) (if (divide x p) (Eq x 1)))) (x => iffI (if (divide x p) (or (Eq x 1) (Eq x p))) (if (Lt x p) (if (divide x p) (Eq x 1))) (h12 => ifI (Lt x p) (if (divide x p) (Eq x 1)) (l => ifI (divide x p) (Eq x 1) (d => Or__resolve_right (Eq x 1) (Eq x p) (ifE (divide x p) (or (Eq x 1) (Eq x p)) h12 d) (Nat__ne_of_lt x p l)))) (h13 => ifI (divide x p) (or (Eq x 1) (Eq x p)) (d2 => Or__imp_left (Lt x p) (Eq x 1) (Eq x p) (ifI (Lt x p) (Eq x 1) (l1 => ifE (divide x p) (Eq x 1) (ifE (Lt x p) (if (divide x p) (Eq x 1)) h13 l1) d2)) (LE__le__lt_or_eq_dec Nat x p (Nat__le_of_dvd x p (Nat__le_of_succ_le (succ 0) p p2) d2))))))))).
English
Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m < p$, then if $m$ divides $p$, then $m = 1$.
French
Soit $p \in \Natural$. Alors $p$ est premier si et seulement si $2 \leq p$ et pour tous les nombres naturels $m$, si $m < p$, alors si $m$ divise $p$, alors $m = 1$.
Czech
Nechť $p \in \Natural$. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, právě tehdy, když $2 \leq p$ a pro všechna přirozená čísla $m$, jestliže $m < p$, pak jestliže $m$ dělí $p$, pak $m = 1$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \mathrm{prime} (p)\leftrightarrow 2 \leq p \wedge (\forall m \in \Natural . m < p \rightarrow \divides {m}{p}\rightarrow m = 1)$

Nat.prime_def_lt'theoremDefs.lean:114

theorem prime_def_lt' {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → m < p → ¬m ∣ p :=
  prime_def_lt.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      forall_congr' fun m =>
        ⟨fun h m2 l d => not_lt_of_ge m2 ((h l d).symm ▸ by decide), fun h l d => by
          rcases m with (_ | _ | m)
          · omega
          · rfl
          · exact (h (le_add_left 2 m) l).elim d⟩
Dedukti
1 lines

Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $2 \leq m$, then if $m < p$, then $m$ does not divide $p$.

Czech

Nat.prime_def_le_sqrttheoremDefs.lean:124

theorem prime_def_le_sqrt {p : ℕ} : Prime p ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ∀ m, 2 ≤ m → m ≤ sqrt p → ¬m ∣ p :=
  prime_def_lt'.trans <|
    and_congr_right fun p2 =>
      ⟨fun a m m2 l => a m m2 <| lt_of_le_of_lt l <| sqrt_lt_self p2, fun a m m2 l mdvd@⟨k, e⟩ => by
        rcases le_sqrt_of_eq_mul e with hm | hk
        · exact a m m2 hm mdvd
        · rw [mul_comm] at e
          exact a k (Nat.lt_of_mul_lt_mul_right (a := m) (by rwa [one_mul, ← e])) hk ⟨m, e⟩⟩

Nat.prime_iff_not_exists_mul_eqtheoremDefs.lean:133

theorem prime_iff_not_exists_mul_eq {p : ℕ} :
    p.Prime ↔ 2 ≤ p ∧ ¬ ∃ m n, m < p ∧ n < p ∧ m * n = p := by
  push Not
  simp_rw [prime_def_lt, dvd_def, exists_imp]
  refine and_congr_right fun hp ↦ forall_congr' fun m ↦ (forall_congr' fun h ↦ ?_).trans forall_comm
  simp_rw [Ne, forall_comm (β := _ = _), eq_comm, imp_false, not_lt]
  refine forall₂_congr fun n hp ↦ ⟨by simp_all, fun hpn ↦ ?_⟩
  have := mul_ne_zero_iff.mp (hp ▸ show p ≠ 0 by lia)
  exact (Nat.mul_eq_right (by lia)).mp
    (hp.symm.trans (hpn.antisymm (hp ▸ Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left _ (by lia))))
Dedukti
1 lines
Nat__prime_iff_not_exists_mul_eq : (p : Elem Nat) -> Proof (iff (prime p) (and (Leq 2 p) (not (exists Nat (m => exists Nat (n => and (Lt m p) (and (Lt n p) (Eq (times m n) p)))))))).
English
Let $p \in \Natural$. Then $p$ is prime, if and only if $2 \leq p$ and it is not the case that there exist natural numbers $m$ and $n$, such that $m < p$, $n < p$ and $m \times n = p$.
French

Czech
Nechť $p \in \Natural$. Tedy $p$ je prvočíselné, právě tehdy, když $2 \leq p$ a není případ že existují přirozená čísla $m$ a $n$, takový, že $m < p$, $n < p$ a $m \times n = p$.
Formula
$p \in \Natural \vdash \mathrm{prime} (p)\leftrightarrow 2 \leq p \wedge \neg (\exists m \in \Natural . \exists n \in \Natural . m < p \wedge (n < p \wedge m \times n = p))$
Proof
13 lines

Nat.prime_of_coprimetheoremDefs.lean:144

theorem prime_of_coprime (n : ℕ) (h1 : 1 < n) (h : ∀ m < n, m ≠ 0 → n.Coprime m) : Prime n := by
  refine prime_def_lt.mpr ⟨h1, fun m mlt mdvd => ?_⟩
  have hm : m ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    rw [zero_dvd_iff] at mdvd
    exact mlt.ne' mdvd
  exact (h m mlt hm).symm.eq_one_of_dvd mdvd

English
Let $n$ be a natural number such that $1 < n$ and for all natural numbers $m$, if $m < n$, then if $m \neq 0$, then $n$ and $m$ are coprime. Then $n$ is prime.
French

Czech

5.We have a contradiction.exact mlt.ne' mdvd
6.$m = 1$.exact (h m mlt hm).symm.eq_one_of_dvd mdvd

If n < k * k, then minFacAux n k = n, if k | n, then minFacAux n k = k. Otherwise, minFacAux n k = minFacAux n (k+2) using well-founded recursion. If n is odd and 1 < n, then minFacAux n 3 is the smallest prime factor of n.
This definition is by well-founded recursion, so rfl or decide cannot be used. One can use norm_num to prove Nat.prime n for small n.

Nat.minFac_le_divtheoremDefs.lean:347
Lean
theorem minFac_le_div {n : ℕ} (pos : 0 < n) (np : ¬Prime n) : minFac n ≤ n / minFac n :=
  match minFac_dvd n with
  | ⟨0, h0⟩ => absurd pos <| by rw [h0, mul_zero]; decide
  | ⟨1, h1⟩ => by
    rw [mul_one] at h1
    rw [prime_def_minFac, not_and_or, ← h1, eq_self_iff_true, _root_.not_true, _root_.or_false,
      not_le] at np
    rw [le_antisymm (le_of_lt_succ np) (succ_le_of_lt pos), minFac_one, Nat.div_one]
  | ⟨x + 2, hx⟩ => by
    conv_rhs =>
      congr
      rw [hx]
    rw [Nat.mul_div_cancel_left _ (minFac_pos _)]
    exact minFac_le_of_dvd (le_add_left 2 x) ⟨minFac n, by rwa [mul_comm]⟩

Nat.minFac_sq_le_selftheoremDefs.lean:364
The square of the smallest prime factor of a composite number n is at most n.
Lean
theorem minFac_sq_le_self {n : ℕ} (w : 0 < n) (h : ¬Prime n) : minFac n ^ 2 ≤ n :=
  have t : minFac n ≤ n / minFac n := minFac_le_div w h
  calc
    minFac n ^ 2 = minFac n * minFac n := sq (minFac n)
    _ ≤ n / minFac n * minFac n := Nat.mul_le_mul_right (minFac n) t
    _ ≤ n := div_mul_le_self n (minFac n)

Nat.minFac_eq_two_ifftheoremDefs.lean:383

@[simp]
theorem minFac_eq_two_iff (n : ℕ) : minFac n = 2 ↔ 2 ∣ n := by
  constructor
  · intro h
    rw [← h]
    exact minFac_dvd n
  · intro h
    have ub := minFac_le_of_dvd (le_refl 2) h
    have lb := minFac_pos n
    refine ub.eq_or_lt.resolve_right fun h' => ?_
    suffices n.minFac = 1 by simp_all
    exact (le_antisymm (Nat.succ_le_of_lt lb) (Nat.lt_succ_iff.mp h')).symm

Nat.coprime_of_dvdtheoremDefs.lean:406

theorem coprime_of_dvd {m n : ℕ} (H : ∀ k, Prime k → k ∣ m → ¬k ∣ n) : Coprime m n := by
  rw [coprime_iff_gcd_eq_one]
  by_contra g2
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hpdvd⟩ := exists_prime_and_dvd g2
  apply H p hp <;> apply dvd_trans hpdvd
  · exact gcd_dvd_left _ _
  · exact gcd_dvd_right _ _

theorem Prime.coprime_iff_not_dvd {p n : ℕ} (pp : Prime p) : Coprime p n ↔ ¬p ∣ n :=
  ⟨fun co d => pp.not_dvd_one <| co.dvd_of_dvd_mul_left (by simp [d]), fun nd =>
    coprime_of_dvd fun _ m2 mp => ((prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq m2 pp).1 mp).symm ▸ nd⟩

Nat.forall_prime_iff_two_and_oddtheoremBasic.lean:110
A property holds for every prime iff it holds for 2 and for every odd prime.
Lean
theorem forall_prime_iff_two_and_odd {P : ℕ → Prop} :
    (∀ p, p.Prime → P p) ↔ P 2 ∧ ∀ p, p.Prime → Odd p → P p := by
  refine ⟨fun h ↦ ⟨h 2 prime_two, fun p hp _ ↦ h p hp⟩, fun h p hp ↦ ?_⟩
  obtain rfl | hne := eq_or_ne p 2
  · exact h.1
  · exact h.2 p hp (hp.odd_of_ne_two hne)

Nat.Prime.not_coprime_iff_dvdtheoremBasic.lean:132
Lean
theorem Prime.not_coprime_iff_dvd {m n : ℕ} : ¬Coprime m n ↔ ∃ p, Prime p ∧ p ∣ m ∧ p ∣ n := by
  apply Iff.intro
  · intro h
    exact
      ⟨minFac (gcd m n), minFac_prime h, (minFac_dvd (gcd m n)).trans (gcd_dvd_left m n),
        (minFac_dvd (gcd m n)).trans (gcd_dvd_right m n)⟩
  · intro h
    obtain ⟨p, hp⟩ := h
    apply Nat.not_coprime_of_dvd_of_dvd (Prime.one_lt hp.1) hp.2.1 hp.2.2

Nat.coprime_of_lt_minFactheoremBasic.lean:143
If 0 < m < minFac n, then n and m are coprime.

lemma coprime_of_lt_minFac {n m : ℕ} (h₀ : m ≠ 0) (h : m < minFac n) : Coprime n m := by
  rw [← not_not (a := n.Coprime m), Prime.not_coprime_iff_dvd]
  push Not
  exact fun p hp hn hm ↦
    ((le_of_dvd (by lia) hm).trans_lt <| h.trans_le <| minFac_le_of_dvd hp.two_le hn).false

Nat.Prime.mul_eq_prime_sq_ifftheoremBasic.lean:181

theorem Prime.mul_eq_prime_sq_iff {x y p : ℕ} (hp : p.Prime) (hx : x ≠ 1) (hy : y ≠ 1) :
    x * y = p ^ 2 ↔ x = p ∧ y = p := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ?_, fun ⟨h₁, h₂⟩ => h₁.symm ▸ h₂.symm ▸ (sq _).symm⟩
  have pdvdxy : p ∣ x * y := by rw [h]; simp [sq]
  -- Could be `wlog := hp.dvd_mul.1 pdvdxy using x y`, but that imports more than we want.
  suffices ∀ x' y' : ℕ, x' ≠ 1 → y' ≠ 1 → x' * y' = p ^ 2 → p ∣ x' → x' = p ∧ y' = p by
    obtain hx | hy := hp.dvd_mul.1 pdvdxy <;>
      [skip; rw [And.comm]] <;>
      [skip; rw [mul_comm] at h pdvdxy] <;>
      apply this <;>
      assumption
  rintro x y hx hy h ⟨a, ha⟩
  have : a ∣ p := ⟨y, by rwa [ha, sq, mul_assoc, mul_right_inj' hp.ne_zero, eq_comm] at h⟩
  obtain rfl | hap := (Nat.dvd_prime hp).mp ‹a ∣ p›
  · rw [mul_one] at ha
    subst ha
    simp only [sq, mul_right_inj' hp.ne_zero] at h
    subst h
    exact ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
  · refine (hy ?_).elim
    subst hap
    subst ha
    rw [sq, Nat.mul_eq_left (Nat.mul_ne_zero hp.ne_zero hp.ne_zero)] at h
    exact h

theorem ne_one_iff_exists_prime_dvd : ∀ {n}, n ≠ 1 ↔ ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ p ∣ n
  | 0 => by simpa using Exists.intro 2 Nat.prime_two
  | 1 => by simp [Nat.not_prime_one]
  | n + 2 => by
    let a := n + 2
    have ha : a ≠ 1 := Nat.succ_succ_ne_one n
    simp only [a, true_iff, Ne, not_false_iff, ha]
    exact ⟨a.minFac, Nat.minFac_prime ha, a.minFac_dvd⟩

theorem succ_dvd_or_succ_dvd_of_succ_sum_dvd_mul {p : ℕ} (p_prime : Prime p) {m n k l : ℕ}
    (hpm : p ^ k ∣ m) (hpn : p ^ l ∣ n) (hpmn : p ^ (k + l + 1) ∣ m * n) :
    p ^ (k + 1) ∣ m ∨ p ^ (l + 1) ∣ n := by
  have hpd : p ^ (k + l) * p ∣ m * n := by rwa [pow_succ] at hpmn
  have hpd2 : p ∣ m * n / p ^ (k + l) := dvd_div_of_mul_dvd hpd
  have hpd3 : p ∣ m * n / (p ^ k * p ^ l) := by simpa [pow_add] using hpd2
  have hpd4 : p ∣ m / p ^ k * (n / p ^ l) := by simpa [Nat.div_mul_div_comm hpm hpn] using hpd3
  have hpd5 : p ∣ m / p ^ k ∨ p ∣ n / p ^ l :=
    (Prime.dvd_mul p_prime).1 hpd4
  suffices p ^ k * p ∣ m ∨ p ^ l * p ∣ n by rwa [_root_.pow_succ, _root_.pow_succ]
  exact hpd5.elim (fun h : p ∣ m / p ^ k => Or.inl <| mul_dvd_of_dvd_div hpm h)
    fun h : p ∣ n / p ^ l => Or.inr <| mul_dvd_of_dvd_div hpn h

theorem Prime.dvd_factorial : ∀ {n p : ℕ} (_ : Prime p), p ∣ n ! ↔ p ≤ n
  | 0, _, hp => iff_of_false hp.not_dvd_one (not_le_of_gt hp.pos)
  | n + 1, p, hp => by
    rw [factorial_succ, hp.dvd_mul, Prime.dvd_factorial hp]
    exact
      ⟨fun h => h.elim (le_of_dvd (succ_pos _)) le_succ_of_le, fun h =>
        (_root_.lt_or_eq_of_le h).elim (Or.inr ∘ le_of_lt_succ) fun h => Or.inl <| by rw [h]⟩

theorem coprime_factorial_iff {m n : ℕ} (hm : m ≠ 1) :
    m.Coprime n ! ↔ n < m.minFac := by
  rw [← not_le, iff_not_comm, Nat.Prime.not_coprime_iff_dvd]
  constructor
  · intro h
    exact ⟨m.minFac, minFac_prime hm, minFac_dvd m, Nat.dvd_factorial (minFac_pos m) h⟩
  · rintro ⟨p, hp, hdvd, hdvd'⟩
    exact le_trans (minFac_le_of_dvd hp.two_le hdvd) (hp.dvd_factorial.mp hdvd')

Nat.exists_infinite_primestheoremInfinite.lean:36
Euclid's theorem on the *infinitude of primes*. Here given in the form: for every n, there exists a prime number p ≥ n.
Lean
theorem exists_infinite_primes (n : ℕ) : ∃ p, n ≤ p ∧ Prime p :=
  let p := minFac (n ! + 1)
  have f1 : n ! + 1 ≠ 1 := ne_of_gt <| succ_lt_succ <| factorial_pos _
  have pp : Prime p := minFac_prime f1
  have np : n ≤ p :=
    le_of_not_ge fun h =>
      have h₁ : p ∣ n ! := dvd_factorial (minFac_pos _) h
      have h₂ : p ∣ 1 := (Nat.dvd_add_iff_right h₁).2 (minFac_dvd _)
      pp.not_dvd_one h₂
  ⟨p, np, pp⟩

theorem pow_minFac {n k : ℕ} (hk : k ≠ 0) : (n ^ k).minFac = n.minFac := by
  rcases eq_or_ne n 1 with (rfl | hn)
  · simp
  have hnk : n ^ k ≠ 1 := fun hk' => hn ((pow_eq_one_iff_left hk).1 hk')
  apply (minFac_le_of_dvd (minFac_prime hn).two_le ((minFac_dvd n).pow hk)).antisymm
  apply
    minFac_le_of_dvd (minFac_prime hnk).two_le
      ((minFac_prime hnk).dvd_of_dvd_pow (minFac_dvd _))
